# Changelog

# 📦 8.2

## 📰 General changes and notes

Summary of changes here!

<details open>
<summary>Github Commits :octocat:</summary>
<blockquote>

- chore: changelogs
- chore: modlist
- chore: changelogs
- fix: improve Turkish quest localization (#4362)
- * fix: align Ars Nouveau quests with Turkish localization
- * fix: align Turkish quest terminology and chapter titles
- * fix: align Turkish logistics and cross-chapter quest terminology
- * fix: align Turkish quest terminology with mod localizations
- * fix: add Auroral Turkish localization and sync quest translations
- * fix: align Turkish quest terminology and correct descriptions
- * Update Turkish translations for Create, Cataclysm, Immersive Engineering, Mekanism & Reactors
- * fix: correct Turkish chapter 1–4 terminology and quest instructions
- * fix: improve Turkish Ice and Fire quest translations
- * fix: improve Turkish quest translations for Aether, EvilCraft, Tips and MI
- * fix: improve Turkish quest translations for AE2
- * fix: improve Turkish quest translations for basic logistics, integrated dynamics, modular router
- * fix: improve Turkish quest translations
- * fix: improve Turkish quest translations for pneumaticcraft, industrial foregoing, just dire things, hostile neural networks
- * fix: improve Turkish quest translations for Draconic evolution, productive bees, railcraft
- * fix: improve Turkish quest translations for productive trees
- Prevent progression bypasses and remove invalid Neo Vitae material integrations (#4370)
- Disable Glyph of Nullify Defense and hide it from recipe viewers (#4372)
- * Disable Glyph of Nullify Defense
- * Hide Glyph of Nullify Defense from Recipe Viewer
- Prevent Shiny mobs from being used in spawners (#4405)
- Prevent Reliquified Artifacts from going into Accessories slots (#4409)
- Ported from All The Mons: https://github.com/AllTheMods/All-the-Mons/commit/67a53a7d830efb9e0e33730ebe296d41ce6acbc5
- Pixie No Pixing: Disable Pixie Item Theft (#4385)
- Blacklist Neo Vitae Imprisonment Array from certain mobs (#4382)
- - The blacklist includes all uncapturable mobs, as per #c:capturing_not_supported, as well as all mobs part of the Apothic Spawners blacklist tag
- [Cross-port] Remove raw materials from angler hat loot (#4388)
- See https://github.com/AllTheMods/All-the-Mons/commit/bff529057904ec79d40e98d36b309a234865105f
- Co-authored-by: Pietro Lopes <97140255+pietro-lopes@users.noreply.github.com>
- Updated PT_BR Localizations (#4389)
- Exclude Ascension from Corail Tombstone's beneficial effect handling (#4402)
- Add Draconic Dragon Heart Drop to Hostile Neural Networks (#4406)
- Block bundles in Tool Belt to prevent item duplication (#4403)
- Add compressed glass smelting recipes for convenience (#4411)
- Remove duplicate silver processing recipes from Mekanism: More Machine (#4416)
- Add Ad Astra oil to the common crude oil tag (#4417)
- chore: bcc
- fix: recipes
- chore: modlist</blockquote>

</details>
